# Architecture refactor plan

This plan brings the compiler and its tools in line with `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`. That document
states the contracts; its §7 "Known deviations" table lists where the tree breaks them. The
fourteen LARGE items and the SMALL groups in `code-quality-2026-10.md` give each deviation its
measurement, its proposed shape and its first PR. This plan owns what neither of them says:
**the order, the dependencies, how a step is accepted, and where each item stands.** It does not
restate measurements; follow the links.

## Redirected, 2026-10-25: one producer per fact

The owner stopped shape-by-shape fixing: L1's closes were filing about one new row each, so
the family never shrank. The program now follows the seven-step migration in
[architecture-target-2026-10.md](architecture-target-2026-10.md) §3. The checker elaborates
each instance once and puts a function value's identity in its type, and the emitter only
lowers. That migration replaces the L1, L2 and ExpCtx parts of this plan wherever they
disagree.

- **Scoreboard:** the speed of `vl check`, `vl run` and `vl build` (diderven check and build
  fuel, plumb-shape cost, L2 self-compile fuel), plus leanness (compiler lines, seed bytes,
  special-case helper counts). The baselines are in §4.1 of that document. Rows close as a
  side effect.
- **No loss of language function, inference first:** no step may require an annotation.
- **A step lands when it loses no `runs` and is a sound move toward the target** (owner,
  2026-10-26: line count is not a bar). Lines, seed bytes and fuel are reported as side
  effects, not landing criteria; the CI gates (seed +3%, plumb-shape, scaling shape) still
  apply. Its proof is the same each time:
  - an unchanged difftree, apart from the intended movers;
  - no `runs → not-runs`;
  - the fuel readings above, reported.
- **Increments and slices land in slices:** each slice is its own PR with its own proof.
- **Paused:** no targeted L1 lanes and no new per-class L2 rules. L6's render-parse work
  continues on its own.

## When the program is done

- Every row of ARCHITECTURE.md §7 is gone, except rows that wait on an owner ruling and say so.
- Every item L1–L14 is closed, or retired with a reason recorded here.
- The ratchets that measure these deviations have reached their floors, and each is a gate:
  - render-parse (`render-parse-budget`);
  - `sentinel-index-unguarded`;
  - `kind-ladder-incomplete` and `kind-ladder-split`;
  - the emitter's never-cleared state (`emitter-state-audit.vl --check`, ratcheted by L7).
- The repo's tooling follows the standing order: everything that can be written in VL is
  written in VL (Track T).

### Scope cut, 2026-10-24

Two items close on a measured floor instead of an exhaustive sweep:

- **L3** is done when every emit refusal that a `vl check`-clean program reaches has
  either moved to the checker (the design forbids the program) or has an open CAPABILITY
  row (the program is legal and cannot be built yet). A refusal site that no check-clean
  program reaches stays as an internal floor, recorded with its reason, and is not work.
- **L2 and L6's render-parse residue** stop at the floor: the renders and parses that are
  the key, the diagnostic or the recorded TypeRef text
  ([render-parse-residue-2026-10.md](render-parse-residue-2026-10.md)). The ratchet's
  baseline at that point is the floor.

Update this table in the PR that moves an item.

## Rules every step follows

1. **One PR is one step.** A refactor step proves it changed nothing:
   - a byte-identical seed (`cmp` after `refresh-compiler.sh`);
   - or, where code moves, IDENTITY (compile(candidate, master source) == master fixpoint,
     which `cb-audit` checks) plus identical `vl check` text and emitted modules over
     `tests/cases` and the distilled corpus.
2. **A step that changes behaviour** must show:
   - no `runs → not-runs` in `regress.py`, and no new silent cell;
   - for a capability, a `capability-matrix.vl` position matrix in both faces, with a template that leaves
     a type parameter open.
3. **Cost is a gate, not a note.** A slower compiler is rejected however clean the code. Each
   step is checked against:
   - L2 self-compile fuel (`self-compile-time.sh`);
   - `plumb-shape-cost.vl` fuel;
   - `vl_scaling_shape_test.ts`;
   - seed size.
4. **Shadow before switch.** Where a new producer replaces an old one (L1, L2 stage 3, L6), a
   shadow harness that is off by default runs first and prints every disagreement. A
   disagreement becomes an inventory row, not a merged behaviour change. The disagreement list
   is the step's deliverable.
5. **A step removes or narrows its §7 line in the same PR**, and updates the status table above.
6. **One writer per giant file at a time.** `typecheck.vl`, `emit_classify.vl`, `wasmEmit.vl`
   and `emit_mono.vl` each have one owning lane per wave (see Concurrency).
7. **Fresh builder per PR**, with a handoff note, and up to six concurrent agents (CLAUDE.md).
   Merges go through `cb-audit`.
8. **A PR that advances a lane changes only that lane's state cell** in the status table and **appends one bullet** to the end of its `### <lane> log` under "Lane logs" (never editing an earlier bullet). The merge tooling unions appends, so two PRs on different lanes never conflict.

## Dependency graph

```
L4 assert ──► L4 CheckMark ──┬──► L5 pinDriver ──┐
                             └────────────────────┴──► L9 typecheck carves
S-c (one ast import) ─────────────────────────────────► L9

L8 tyquery.vl ──► L2 stage 2 twins ──┐
                                     ├──► L2 stage 3 rep column ──► RepDesc resume
L1 (SC steps 0→4) ───────────────────┤        ▲
                                     │        └── rulings: literal-union rep, nullable rep
L6 registries by id ─────────────────┘   (removes L1's spelling hop)

L1 SC step 4 ──► L3 family 3 (mono fn-value rules into the checker)
L7 ExpCtx deliveries ──► per-instance compilation (vl test isolation ruling)

T1 host process ABI (done) ──► T3 instrument ports ──► Python retired
L11 budgets inside `vl lint` (needs no spawn) ─► T3 drops the ratchet scripts
owner: optimizer location ──► L10
```

A wave is a set of steps whose prerequisites have all landed. Steps inside a wave run in any
order, subject to the one-writer rule.

## Concurrency

Up to six agents run at once (CLAUDE.md); the owner lifted the CPU cap on 2026-10-12. Rule 6
still holds: one writer per giant file.

- **Lane 1: SC.** B1, then C1(d). It owns `emit_mono.vl` and its parts of `typecheck.vl`
  throughout.
- **The other lanes** each take one step at a time from the earliest wave with an open step,
  choosing a step whose file has no current writer. The intended order per file is:
  - A1 → A2 → C2 → D1 (one continuous claim on `typecheck.vl`, interleaved with SC by area);
  - A3 → B2 → D2 → D3 (`emit_classify.vl`);
  - C3 (`wasmEmit.vl`);
  - A4, A5, A6, A7, B3 and T2, which go to whichever file is free.
- T3 ports need no new std surface, since T1 shipped. A port that does need some goes through std-api review first.

## Owner decisions this plan needs

Each is posted for question time; work that does not depend on it continues.

1. **`one-literal-union-rep`**: RULED 2026-10-13 and applied (#3653): every string literal union reps as the atom.
2. **`nullable-rep-rule-stated-once`**: RULED 2026-10-13, (b): `repOfNullable` becomes a `_`-less `match`.
3. **Where the `-O` optimizer lives (L10).** RULED 2026-10-13, (A): in VL (`optimizer-in-vl.md`), as a pass over the emitted
   module in the compiler. That is what the standing order asks. It also gives the Deno and
   playground hosts `-O`, which they cannot run today, and it removes about 6.5k lines of Rust
   that the host's own manifest says it does not have. If decided this way, skip L10's Rust-only
   first PR (`wasm_bytes`).
4. **September row 19**: a development build resolves the seed from the working directory but
   std from the executable's tree (`SeedOrigin::Cwd`). Recommendation: resolve both from the
   executable's tree.
5. **SC ruling (g)**: the multi-pin case (a binding called at two or more signatures), after the
   table lands. This decides B1's instance record for closures, not whether B1 happens.
6. **Sibling contextual typing in list literals** (D12800): `fs.push((y) => y * 2)` onto a list of hole functions runs, while the same lambda as a literal element (`[(x: i32) => x + 1, (y) => y * 2]`) is a check reject, because a list literal's element type is the join of its elements (E15.2). That face is DESIGN today; making it agree with the push face is a language change.

## Ready to brief

These have no unmet prerequisite and touch disjoint files:

- **A1**: L4 assertion harness (`typecheck.vl`). Done (#3600).
- **A2**: L4 `CheckMark` / `checkRollback` (`typecheck.vl`), on A1. Done (#3605), with the leak fixes in #3609.
- **A5**: errno agreement test (host + `std/fs.vl`).
- **A6 / S-c**: one `./ast` import block in `typecheck.vl`. This conflicts with A1, so run it
  first; it is small.
- **C3 first PR**: `emitReturnValue` through `ExpCtx` (`wasmEmit.vl`).
- **B1**: continues in lane SC; steps 1 to 3 are done, and step 4's two container gates (the list pin's and the record-field pin's) are retired (L1 log, 2026-10-19). What is left of step 4 is the open rows its retirements filed.
- **T3, first port — done**: `check-filed-witnesses.vl` prints the Python's report byte for byte on both inventories and now runs the `filed witnesses` gate row; the Python is deleted and its test pins the report. It found D4290 (a compiler trap) and one new `dogfood:` row (`runProgram` waits). Next: `regress.py` + `gradecensus.py`.

- **B2, the rest (step 6): an identifier narrowing as a zero-length path.** Census on
  2026-10-29, after #5124 and #5125 removed the bank's type column. Today an identifier
  narrowing writes a scope layer (`declareSlot`, 7 sites: `check_narrow_flow.vl` 894, 2245,
  4093; `check_node_rules.vl` 1603, 1620; `check_operators.vl` `declareAt`, 2 calls;
  `check_capture_widen.vl` 181) and a bank row (`noteNarShadow`, the same sites), and
  `check_elab_cap.vl` 223 restores layers from a snapshot. About 26 references walk the
  layers (`slotLayerTop`/`slotLayerPrev`, `crIsNarrowLayer`, `scopeVisibleSnapshot`), and
  about ten functions read the bank rows. The place store a path narrowing writes is read in 42
  functions; seven of them walk every row (`noteCallInvalidations`, `elemEverySlotOf`,
  `retireNarrowingsForWrite`, `retireIndexedNarrowings`, `retireAliasedNarrowings`,
  `crLoopPathPreRetire`, and `crNoteRhsRetired`, which matches one path), and the rest
  index a slot `npSlotOf` or a mark already found. So
  the staging is:
  1. Each scan that walks every row skips a place that is not a path. No behaviour change.
  2. Each narrowing site pushes an identifier-place row beside its bank row, with the
     bank's kind and epoch as columns. The bank's readers move onto those rows one function
     at a time, `crCallCandidates`' stamp cache last. Then the bank is deleted.
  3. Identifier reads take a narrowed type from the row rather than the layer. The 7 writers
     stop writing a layer, and the join's snapshot covers both kinds of place. This is where
     a read costs `npSlotOf`'s scan, so it needs a per-declaration index first. The
     scaling-shape gate is the check.

  Each stage lands only with 0 differences over the 17,265-file comparison and with scaling
  shape green.

The drop decision's key ladder (`calleeCloSigKeyAt`) returned the initializer's type key unconditionally, and the generic's open `(x: T) => void` has none, so an empty key read as a value; the call itself was keyed `i>v` from the callee's own type. The initializer rung and the copy rung now answer only with a non-empty key and otherwise fall through to that type. Thirteen sibling spellings ran before and after: annotated return, non-void generic, void non-generic, binding and argument delivery and call in place ran on both; local, copy, `let`, capture, lambda, nested return and two-parameter were invalid wasm on master and run now. `fn-value-generic-instance` runs 46 of 48 graded cells, up from 45, with 0 runs → not-runs and 0 silent; `map_value`/`map_set` are D4579. D5530: where the checker bound no instance at the hole, `monoHoleGenFnFlowPin` delivers a generic that declares type parameters at the one pin the flow table gives its read past the call, types the calls taking it, and returns the type they agree on to the forwarding parameter. `ffDirect` keys a call handing in an open generic signature, at a hole or a type parameter, by its own site (`ffOpenSigSiteKey`), so two `fw(k)` take two instances, and so does `sel(id)` through a generic identity (D4121, D4122). A generic only by its un-annotated parameters is left to the existing refusal, which `error-generic-fn-value-inferred-hof.vl` pins. D5531: `mk` takes no argument, so it is walked once and its return slot carries both results' pins; it needs `mk` instanced per result pin. - D5531 closes; D5660–D5664 are filed open. This is D3975's shape, a per-call-type instance stored by value, and it is built as a `fn_split` copy per result type, not as a per-call-type return record. A copy allocates nothing, keeps every call direct, and costs a program that is never refused nothing, because the split runs only after a refusal asks for it. A record would add a field read to each call through the result. `monoCoerceRetAtPin` asks `fsAsk` when a refused return has more than one pin past it. `fsPlanFn` then groups the function's calls by result key: the slot keys of the binding a result initializes, or the call's own key. Each key past the first gets a reparsed copy (`reparseFuncDeclAt`, `parser.vl`), and its calls are renamed. The function must be at module scope and called only, never read as a value. One value called at two types, `const a = fw(k)` with `fw` an identity (`fsIdentityArg`), gives the binding a slot per type. The slots past the first hold the identity call's argument, so the call still runs once, in the first slot. The ask comes from the value read's refusal (`wasmEmit.vl`) and from the monomorphizer's un-annotated-argument refusal. Both grading lines run: the alias prints `1 s` and `fw(k)` prints `1 z`. Ten sibling spellings that master refuses run now, among them three types through one maker, a loop, `if`/`else` returning two generics, and `fw` printing first. Seven are still refused. Five are filed: one result called at two types (D5660), a maker with an un-annotated parameter (D5661), a block-local maker (D5662), a maker also read as a value (D5663), and a second identity binding beside the split one (D5664). Of #4000's two sibling rows, the forwarded hole called at two signatures still refuses ("no function type to key a signature from"), and the generic through a generic identity into two bindings already runs on master, as does the relay/`id2`/`inner` spelling. D5472 is a checker literal-row-per-instance error and not this family. Against master `d9c943595`: the new matrix `generic-two-result-pins-inferred` runs 62 of 66 graded cells, up from 0 (64 emit rejects, 2 check rejects). `generic-alias-value-inferred`, `generic-bare-return-inferred` and `generic-hole-forward-inferred` stay at 62 of 66, and `generic-lambda-hole-inferred` stays at 66 of 66. All five lose 0 runs, with 0 silent; the cells left are `map_value`/`map_set` (D4579) and `map_value_place`. An A/B over the 1,664 `closures`, `functions`, `generics` and `inference` cases moves only the new fixture. The seed is 6,041,095 bytes, +7,910.

Failing that, it takes its own signature instanced (`bindGenWalk`, `substTyDeep`) at the one closed pin the flow table carries past the call; the pin is found frame-aware through `monoDeclPathPins` and read by `fnFlowDeclPathPinTysOf` (`fn_flow_shadow.vl`). - D6205 and D6206 close; D6320, D6321 and D6322 are filed open, and D6200 stays open. When no other plan takes the asks, `fsPlanInstances` (`fn_split.vl`) takes each asked module maker (the function holding the refused generic read, and every other module function reading the same generic, since the emitter stops at the first) or asked module `const` of an identity call as a dependency, closes the set over functions that read one and are called at one key, and gives each function reading a dependency and called at two or more keys a copy per key past the first. Each call is renamed to its key's copy, and each copy reads a copy of every dependency of its own, made recursively for a maker reading another. The plan sees only the original parse, so a rename inside a copy is recorded as (copy, source offset, old name, new name) and `fsApplyCopyRenames` applies it to the copy `fsApply` mints from a fresh parse, matching the identifier by offset and name. Lane 0 keeps the originals where no other body has claimed them (`fsInstanceClaim`), because an unread `const q = fw(id)` is itself refused. Siblings that master refuses and that run now: 19 of 24, among them three types, a direct `mk()(u)`, two maker calls, return position, an exported body, a hole body, a loop, a named argument, two bodies sharing a maker, and both rows through a generic left open. Still failing as on master: a block-local maker (D6322), a maker result in a record field (an object literal with no declared type, refused at one instance too), a written `let` (declined), `const q = id` (D6320, clause 1) and a module read beside the body (D6321, clause 1). Against master `5e3ba8429`, with 0 runs → not-runs and 0 → silent in all fifteen matrices, the new `maker-of-generic-in-generic-body-two-instances` runs 66 of 66, up from 0, and the fourteen existing ones are unchanged. The seed is 6,402,865 bytes, +9,333 over master.

- D6472, D6481, D6523, D6471, D6473 and D3927 close; D6700-D6707 are filed open. All six were clause 2, and D3955 (clause 1) closes with D6472. D6472: `fsConstLets` (`fn_split.vl`) spells an un-annotated, unexported, never-written `let` holding a lambda or a function for good as `const`, so every alias reader follows it. D6481: a hole parameter read only as a callee and handed one module function or `const` lambda by name at every call is planned as that function, its calls made by name and the parameter dropped. D6523: the type-parameter plan takes a call of a binding annotated with the parameter, and `fsRenameTypeRef` drops the annotation's parsed spelling tree, which every resolver read before the renamed name; that is why the naive admission had built `mid` at one `F`. D6471: `ffOpenSigSiteKey` (`fn_flow_shadow.vl`) keys an open signature inside a widened record by call site, and `monoFnlessArgsOf` (`emit_mono.vl`) treats an argument delivered to a function-free closed record parameter as inert. D6473: `fsFarCalls` follows a returned or listed record to its field calls at the kept types. D3927: `fnOwnerKey` (`check_subst.vl`) owns a nested function's type variables by name and node, so two nested `inner<U>` keep two pins of `U`.

The declaring frame owns it in its env-cache slot, allocated at first use and reset at the declaration statement. The cell rides, as the capture `#cell:<fe>`, every env between that frame and a reader (`capCellNote`, `capRecord`, emit_classify.vl). Every value read fills the cell if it is empty and returns its closure (`emitCloCellRef`, wasmEmit.vl). A loop body's fresh declaration is a fresh cell, and another call of the declaring function is a new value. A cell's function is never lifted (`cloLiftDecide`).

### L4 log

- State on 2026-10-14: A1 and A2 **done**
- A1 (#3600) is the assertion and its measured list.
- A2 (#3605) adds `CheckMark` / `checkRollback` and records of columns, with each site's set unchanged and the census identical.
- The follow-up (#3609) widened each leaking site's set, one commit per site: over 15,288 programs no rc, diagnostic or emitted module moves, and the census leaves only `cstrProbeBefore`'s constraint rows, kept by design (D3062).
- `nestCstrSeen` now trusts a key only while its row stands; `tpIfOkAt` (a syntactic fact), `dJoinRelayed` (a per-type mark) and `tpIfJoinAt` (the last apart join seen at a node, as before A2) are kept across a rollback.
- The keyed maps roll back. Census first: a census seed counting every keyed map's key count at each probe (`cfd99f293`, `tests/cases` and the distilled corpus) found 104 maps a probe writes. The three named maps were left grown by these sites:

  | map | site | programs | example |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | `tpIfJoinAt` | `globalInitPrePass` | 2 | `generics/if-join-two-list-types-union.vl` |
  | `dJoinRelayed` | `cstrProbeBefore`, `inferReturnsPrePass` | 2 | `numerics/recursive-generic-joins-runtime-numbers-apart.vl` |
  | `tpIfOkAt` | `globalInitPrePass` | 42 | `generics/coalesce-join-at-list-pin.vl` |
  | `tpIfOkAt` | `solveUnannotParams` | 6 | `generics/enclosing-param-into-generic-nullable.vl` |
  | `tpIfOkAt` | `inferReturnsPrePass` | 4 | `std/idtable-plumb-witness.vl` |
  | `tpIfOkAt` | `monoRecheck` | 4 | `maps/for-in-union-of-maps.vl` |
  | `tpIfOkAt` | `cstrProbeBefore`, `tpIfRecheck` | 3 each | `generics/fn-result-null-into-generic-nullable.vl` |

  To find which stale entries an output reads, an experiment build snapshotted a group of maps at every `checkMark` and restored it at every `checkRollback`, whatever the site's set, and `difftree.sh --identity` compared it with the base (16,534 files, 11,684 built):

  | group | maps | differ | reading |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | g1 | `tpIfJoinAt tpIfOkAt dJoinRelayed` | 0 | stale entries harmless; now rolled back |
  | g2 | `discardValTyAt ifNoValueAt ifValTyAt valDiscardAt` | 0 | per-node facts the kept pass writes again |
  | g4 | `crsTop` and the eight `crw*` write-scan maps | 0 | memos keyed by node |
  | g5 | the fourteen `cr*` capture banks and memos, `cvuSlotOf`, `npKillSure` | 0 | memos keyed by node |
  | g3 | `fnDeclParent letDeclFn wrDstDecl ctnParamArgs ctnParamAt ctnScanAt ctnScanGets ctnWrRecv aliasRootMap extDeclIx sidMap scopeDepthsOf` | 3,292 (8,529 built) | declaration indices; restored, calls name an unknown function |
  | g8 | 29 indices and memos (`cwBiSlot`, `wePartIx`, `igByName`, `luWriteTgt`, `njGateMemo`, `nestParentOf`, `shMemo`, `asgDeclRowsOf`, …) | 52 (11,641 built) | `closures/litunion-mix-closure-result.vl`, `generics/hole-field-write-lands-at-pin.vl`, 44 named `d3204_*` cells |
  | g7 | `annotNameMemo apartHoleJoins efLeaf fvaNameDecl narIfEnds paramNullJoins holeTpJoinNames joinByWant tpJoinUnions tvElseBase tvElseTested holePinSet tyVarOwner holeElseTested annUnionTys` | 1 | `numerics/recursive-generic-joins-runtime-numbers-apart.vl` runs on the base and traps the build (rc 70) restored |
  | g6 | `cexFolded cexNegOperand cexNotTree cexWideFloatOk lbiCandOf lbiInitTy lbiInlined lbiInlinedDecl lbiInlinedName cmpConstLit matchConstPatLit` | 1 | `functions/lambda-inferred-nonref-return.vl` reports its redundant-type hint twice restored |

  Five more were read rather than measured: `solvedParamTys` is what `solveUnannotParams` probes for, `paramSolveTried` says a body is probed at most once, and `weSums`, `weShapeSums` and `weUnresSums` are write-effect summaries with their own settle and drop rule (D6130). So 29 maps keep only entries no output reads, and 72 are read after the rollback on purpose, which §4.3 says a probe should do by reading before the rollback; the §7 row keeps that number. No output witness exists for the three named maps, since g1 moves nothing.

  The mechanism is `compiler/check_map_undo.vl`, an undo log of `(map id, key, had, old value)` rows. `CheckMark.undo` opens it (`undoOpen`), and writes to the three maps go through `undoSetI32`/`undoSetBool`, which log only while a mark is open. `tpIfTruncate` replays `tpIfJoinAt` and `dJoinTruncate` replays `dJoinRelayed`, so each follows its family's sets, which cover every site the census found for them. `checkRollback` replays `tpIfOkAt` at every site, because the pass that keeps a node marks it again. The outermost close empties the log. A replay sets each key's oldest logged value back and, when the probe added a key, returns a copy of the map without it, rather than calling `delete`: the compiler's first `m.delete` brought in the map compaction family and grew every `__map_set_N__`, 23 KB of seed and +0.75% diderven check fuel. The three maps moved into `check_tp_join.vl` beside the families that replay them, and `PRB_TABLES` counts them, so `tests/vl_probe_rollback_test.ts` holds seven of the programs above to no keyed map grown; a build whose replay returns early fails it, naming each one.

  Results: the census over 16,797 programs leaves only `cstrProbeBefore`'s constraint rows. `difftree.sh --identity`: 0 of 16,534 files differ (11,684 built); `regress.vl` no cell changed class; fixpoint identical. Seed 5,786,171 to 5,789,438 bytes. Diderven `vl check src/game.vl` fuel (`VL_FUEL=1 VL_JOBS=1 VL_COMPILE_CACHE=0`), base fixpoint against the candidate: 1,863,268,564 to 1,863,054,547 (-0.01%).
- 2026-10-24, the residue closes by class, not by rollback. The 103 maps a probe still leaves written are not leaks that happen to be harmless: restoring them drops a fact the standing pass reads. Each experiment build was bisected to one map:

  | group | map | reading |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | g7 (trap) | `apartHoleJoins` | keyed by the union row `joinRetTys` makes; the arena row stands, so dropping its column traps the build (rc 70) |
  | g6 (double hint) | `lbiCandOf` | dedup index over `lbiCands`, which no probe truncates; restored, the standing pass declares the candidate twice |
  | g8 (45 of 46) | `asgTvWrite` | twin of the node column `asgNarTyIx` (`recordAsgTvWrite` writes both) |
  | g8 (1 of 46) | `luPosByKey` | index over `cUnionTyIxs`, synced by `luIxCursor`; restoring it alone desyncs the two |

  One real leak: `notePinnedHole` says it never banks a pin from a quiet probe, but `dstTyParamAdapts` probes without `inferQuiet` and banked `holePinSet` pins in 27 programs. `holePinHeld` now holds banking across that probe, and the instrumented census shows no `holePinSet` row after it. No output read the stale pins, so no row is filed.

  Every keyed map in the import closure of the probe sites (222 in 135 modules) carries a class and a reason in `scripts/probe-map-classes.tsv`: `undo` 3, `node-column` 26, `type-column` 11, `table-index` 13, `memo` 46 (with #4690's `pgrOwnerGeneric`, read, not censused), `rewrite` 6, `probe-result` 2, `held` 1 (`holePinSet`), `unwritten` 114. `tests/vl_probe_rollback_test.ts` derives the closure from the tree and fails on an unclassified map, a stale row, an unknown class or a row with no reason, and checks the `undo` rows against the undo log's maps. ARCHITECTURE §4.3 states the classes and the §7 row is deleted.

  Results: `difftree.sh --identity` 0 of 16,534 files differ (11,684 built), fixpoint identical; seed 5,786,875 to 5,786,934 bytes.

### L5 log

- State on 2026-10-14: C2 **done**
- `compiler/check_pins.vl` (#3611) runs three phases — uncalled-export parameters (D3817), record width (#3516, folded in as a phase with its 8-round budget), literal bindings with record adoption — through one `pinRun` with a per-phase `pinBudget`.
- D3817 reads what `solveUnannotParams` observed in its walk instead of its own scoped AST walk.
- `vl check` text, emitted modules and the rollback census identical; the driver's re-check for generated walkers is unchanged.
- Next: D1 (callee-closed carves of `typecheck.vl`).
- D1 needs nothing more from L5: L9 carved `typecheck.vl` to 429 lines, `checkNode` and its dispatcher, one strongly connected component with no callee-closed set left. The next step the L5 proposal names is one refusal queue. The literal-binding, record-adoption and D3817 solvers each kept a message/node queue, a dedup scan and a report loop. They now push rows tagged with a `PinSolver` literal union into `compiler/check_pin_refusals.vl`. A re-check drops only its own solver's rows, the D3817 rows outlive it as before, and `pinReport` raises the rows grouped by solver in the old report order (lbi, forced adoption, adoption, unpinned overload, export parameter). `lbiReport`, `recAdReport`, `expInfReport`, `recAdHasErr` and five queue columns are deleted. `difftree.sh --identity`: 0 of 15,303 files differ (10,349 built), fixpoint identical. L2 fuel 89,183,334,308 (master) to 89,220,256,238 (+0.04%). Seed 5,965,851 bytes. The hint queues stay separate because the host reads the D3817 hint columns through `expInfLineAt` and its siblings.
- One hint queue. Measured first: the pin solvers raise hints at ONE site (D3817's `expInfHintAdd`, seven column pushes), and the driver applies one hint rule (`redunTruncate(0)` on a program with errors, D3800); lbi, record adoption and the width solver raise none, and the walk's own hints (`redun*`, `rcoal*`, `isval*`) stay where the walk raises them. `compiler/check_pin_hints.vl` now holds the rows, tagged by `PinSolver` like the refusal queue: `pinHint` takes the span from the name token and dedups by solver and token, `pinHintDrop` drops one solver's rows, `pinHintReport` holds the D3800 rule. The message is spelled when the row is queued; the host ABI (`expInfCount`, `expInfLineAt` and its siblings) reads the queue, so `entry.vl`, the CLI and the oracle are unchanged. Seven `expInf*` columns are deleted from `check_state.vl`. `vl check` over `tests/cases`, base seed against head: 31,433 lines byte-identical (3,954 hints, 5 of them D3817's). `jwSecondPass` is untouched.
- `jwSecondPass` stays out of the driver. Decision rule: join only with difftree identity 0 differ and L2 fuel within +0.5%. What it does that the loop does not: it lexes and parses generated walker source onto the token stream, splices it into the root, rewrites each deep-`is` site (`jwRewriteAll`, whose refusals are positioned type errors), and returns a parse or type rc. Its re-check is a whole fresh `checkProgram`: every phase again and a second `pinReport`. A `checkProgramAgain` keeps the queues and runs no phase. All four callers (`compileSrcOnce`, `checkSrc`, `checkSrcSym`, `modCompile`) run it only when `P.diags` and `T.diags` are empty after `checkProgram` returns, so after `pinReport` has raised the queued refusals. Joining as a fourth `PinPhase` inside the loop would run before `pinReport`, and two witnesses change output. In `gate_both`, D3817's uncalled-export refusal plus a deep-`is` arm that writes its receiver gives one error today, because the refusal is still queued when a loop phase would run. A phase would also raise the rewrite refusal. In D5490's witness, today's pass 2 drops D3817's hint (its `expInfReset`) and reports `const y: i32 = x` as redundant, and following that hint makes the program rc 1. A loop phase would keep the hint, which is a fix, not a refactor. The join that keeps output identical moves the tail into `checkProgram` as a stage after `pinReport` that calls `checkProgram` again. It needs the checker to import the parser and the driver's lexing (`vcLoadToks`, `vcParseBlocked`, `vcDgPush`) and unifies nothing, so it was not built. No candidate was built, so neither difftree nor fuel was run, and fuel does not decide the question. The `expInf*At` readers still index the hint queue directly. A fix for D5490 that keeps D3817's rows across pass 2 must keep D3817 the only solver raising hints, or those readers must filter by solver.
- D5490 closed. `checkProgram` is now `expInfReset` plus `checkProgramResumed`, and `jwSecondPass` calls the second, because pass 1's D3817 annotations are already in the spliced tree, so its hint rows and `expInfPinLets` are still the right answer. The readers' contract is the assertion, not a filter: the hint queue's solver column has its own type, `HintSolver = "expParam"`, so a second solver raising a hint has to widen that type, and the comment there says the readers owe a filter when it does. A filter would turn each `expInf*At(i)` into a scan for the i-th matching row. - `jwSecondPass` is gone: the deep-`is` walkers are the last stage of `checkProgram` (`pinDeepIs`, `check_pins.vl`), after `pinReport`. The stage runs once per check, only when the checker recorded a deep-`is` site and neither `P.diags` nor `T.diags` holds an entry, which is the condition all four driver callers tested. It calls `jwExpand` (`json_walk.vl`: generate, lex, parse, splice, rewrite; it returns the generated tail's first token, or -1 on a parse or rewrite refusal), then `checkRestart` and the resumed driver, so D3817's rows still outlive the re-check (D5490). The lexing helpers the expansion needs (`vcLoadToks`, `vcParseBlocked`, `vcDgPush` and the tokenless-diag tables) moved from `driver.vl` to `compiler/tok_load.vl`, so the checker does not import the driver. `modCompile` holds the stage off for a bank-served check (`pinDeepIsHold`), and the banks decline a check where it ran (`pinDeepIsRan`), as they declined one with deep-`is` sites before. The re-check stays whole, and it is still not a fourth `PinPhase`, for the reasons in the entry above and one more: the rewrite binds each site's receiver to a new `__vljvN` local inside the arms and the generated walkers are new top-level functions, so scopes, captures and the collect passes change, and the checker's side tables are whole-program and append-only. A partial re-check needs incremental checking, which no lane owns. Two edges move, both on programs that never reach emit: a program with a `__memory_shared__` call outside `std` and a deep-`is` site can now report a rewrite refusal beside that error, because the stage runs before `checkMemSharedOrigin`; and in module mode such a program skips `wiBankWhole`, and `ihFinish` reads the state after the re-check. `difftree.sh --identity`: 0 of 16,534 files differ (11,684 built), fixpoint identical; the seed refreshes to itself and is 5,785,549 bytes against 5,785,616. No fuel win: the compiler and diderven have no deep-`is` site, and a program with one still checks twice.

The seed is byte-identical and output identity holds. Next: derive the compiler's per-slot predicates (`fsArgIsU8List` … `fsSlotTouchesMemory`) from one signature table.
- Owner ruling 2026-10-15 (a): the host answers `EPERM` for raw errno 1 and `EACCES` for 13. `permission_errno` reads the raw errno under `PermissionDenied`, and every other platform answer stays `EACCES`. `STD_ONLY` in the agreement test is empty. A Rust unit test pins the mapping, and `tests/vl_std_fs_permission_test.ts` asks the OS for both: a write to `/proc/self/uid_map` gives EPERM, and a read of a mode-000 file gives EACCES.
- `FS_FLOOR` in `compiler/check_intrinsics.vl` is the compiler's one statement of the floor: one row per slot (name, signature in `HOST_FLOOR`'s spelling, `memory` flag). `fsIntrinsicSlot`, `fsIntrinsicNameAt`, `fsIntrinsicArity`, `fsArgIsU8List`, `fsArgIsI64`, `fsRetIsU8List`, `fsRetIsI64`, `fsRetIsVoid` and `fsSlotTouchesMemory` read it instead of seven hand-kept `if` chains. The checker's 17 `declare` rows and the builtin list's 17 `blPush` rows are each one loop over it, so `tests/vl_host_floor_test.ts` holds three sources rather than five: the table's names to `HOST_FLOOR` and the Deno stubs, its signatures to `HOST_FLOOR`, and it refuses a floor name spelled by hand in `check_core_state.vl` or `driver.vl`. Rust is unchanged apart from a doc comment. Fixpoint holds (5,855,200 bytes, +537). Output identity holds over 15,267 files (10,312 built, 0 differ, 0 uncompared). The distilled corpus lost no runs; its three `trap_loads → trap_loads` movements are the ones master shows. Next: `HOST_FLOOR`'s signature column read from `FS_FLOOR` at host build time, which retires the last cross-language copy.
- `HOST_FLOOR` rows are now (name, handler). `scripts/vl-host/build.rs` parses the `FS_FLOOR` rows of `compiler/check_intrinsics.vl` into `FLOOR_SIGS` in `OUT_DIR` (the build fails on an unparsable row or a missing file), `register_fs_imports` reads the signature from it, and the host build id and CI's host-binary cache key now include that file. Rust tests `floor_tests` refuse a name only one table holds and carry a control that must report three drifts; `tests/vl_host_floor_test.ts` refuses a signature literal in `HOST_FLOOR`. All 17 (name, signature) pairs equal the pre-change hand-written column, and the 36 host-backed fs/args/process/errno/run tests pass on the rebuilt binary. Next: the Deno stubs.
- The Deno harness's 16 hand-listed stub names are gone: `tests/support/hostFloor.ts` parses the `FS_FLOOR` rows of `compiler/check_intrinsics.vl` at load (`parseFloorRows`, which throws on a malformed row or a repeated name) and `runWasm.ts` stubs `FLOOR_NAMES`, with `__proc_exit__` still defined after the spread. Read at load rather than generated, because `runWasm.ts` runs only under Deno with read access and the browser host provides no floor. JS stubs need no signature, so names are all that derive. `tests/vl_host_floor_test.ts` uses the same parser, refuses a floor name spelled as a string in `runWasm.ts` and a harness handler for a name the table lacks, and now carries seven failing controls, one per check, on perturbed copies of the four sources. No `compiler/*.vl` change, so the seed is untouched.

Output identity holds over 15,267 files (10,312 built, 0 differ). The distilled corpus lost no runs, and its three `trap_loads → trap_loads` movements show on master's seed as well. The scan, ladder and sentinel totals are unchanged (77, 341 + 6, 461). One scan, nine ladder findings and twelve sentinel reads moved into the new modules. `tests/vl_host_floor_test.ts` now reads the `declare` rows from `check_core_state.vl`. Under `VL_COMPILE_GC=null`, the candidate and master both trap at the 4 GiB heap (peak RSS 4.06 GiB each).
  - Rebased onto master at #3884. #3881's `narListDeclReads` and its reader `narListReadsDecl` live in `check_core_state.vl` with the other lets, and `checkProgramPass` resets the table through a setter.
- Enabler for the hook: the closure-env parameter is per function. Before this, one function value anywhere in a program moved every function onto the env convention.
  - A function now carries the env only when it is lifted or captures (`fnHasEnvParam`, `emit_classify.vl`). Direct calls to every other function use the plain signature and push no null env.
  - An address-taken function without an env is reached through a thunk `(structref env, params…) → return_call f(params…)`. The thunk is declared with the function's `$fnsig` and sits in table slot `gImports + f`, so closure ids, `call_indirect` and funcref equality are unchanged. `return_call` keeps recursion through a value at constant stack.
  - Why a thunk and not the env on every address-taken function: the address-taken set is known only after the bodies are emitted, but a direct call's signature is fixed when its caller is emitted. A thunk takes nothing away from direct calls and cannot under-approximate the set.
  - Exports no longer need the #1265 ABI wrapper: an export names its function directly, and an exported function that has an env is an `emitFail` floor. `blockHasCallRef` no longer reserves the call-ref scratch local for a direct call, which had put an unused local in about 8,659 functions of a compiler with one function value.
  - D3220 moved. Its hole callee called with an extra argument was invalid wasm only because of the env push. Without the push it printed a wrong value, so the direct call now refuses an operand surplus (`directArgsOverflow`), and the row is a loud emit reject.
  - Hook experiment: a temporary `const abiHook: (i32) => i32 = abiId` in `driver.vl`. With master's convention the seed grew 3.1% (5,854,565 to 6,035,028 bytes); with this one it grows 0.21% (to 5,867,104). The built compiler's fuel compiling the hooked source fell from 100,841,253,124 to 99,444,291,183, the same as the unhooked compiler compiling that source (99,444,291,164), so the convention now costs nothing.
  - What is left for the hook: about 12.5 KB of `$fnsig` types, because one is interned for every value-eligible signature (the rec group grows from 158 to 1,170 types), and the types are emitted before the bodies decide the address-taken set. Separately, the compiler does about 5.4% more compile work on any source that holds a function value. That cost is analysis, not convention, and master's seed pays it too.
  - The seed grows 2,744 bytes (5,855,200 to 5,857,944, +1.1% on the size baseline, no rewrite). L2 self-compile fuel moves from 96,497,775,008 to 96,559,073,490 (+0.06%). Two refreshes give the same seed. The distilled corpus lost no runs and moved nothing to silent; its five `rv3310_*` `trap_loads → trap_loads` message movements show on master's seed as well. The filed witnesses read 2843 of 2843 as filed. A new fixture, `tests/cases/closures/env-param-only-where-read.vl`, runs the same on master and covers a struct field, a generic, a returned value, recursion through a value, an export, a block-local function and a capture.
- The hook was built and not landed; D5017 blocks it. `check_core_state.vl` held two fn-typed slots, `checkDeclFn` and `deliverExprFn`, installed by `checkProgramPass`; the four force edges called through them, and with those edges cut the core is 88 declarations and 10,475 lines.
  - With the hook, `vl check compiler/entry.vl` refuses two reads of `e.asMode` in `emitAsCast` (`wasmEmit.vl`): "the call to `asExprIsUnionCastIn` can write it". The deliver slot alone is enough. The path runs `exprUnion` → `substHoleTyReal` → `opSelfFnKeyFor` → `checkNode` → `holeAssignOk`, and the slot call is a call through a function value.
  - `callDeclFnIx` resolves a function value only when it is a binding bound once to a lambda, so a slot holding a named function makes the summary "may write anything". The same refusal reaches user programs on master, even through a `const` bound to a named function (D5017's witness and ablation).
  - Hoisting `asMode` into locals in `emitAsCast` would build, but it hides the false kill rather than fixing it. The fix is a behaviour change, and so it needs its own PR: either the store set of a module fn binding (its initialiser plus every value a setter can store), or the union of the write shapes of every address-taken function.
- D5017 closed. A call through a binding is charged the union of every function stored into it: the initialiser, each `name = v`, and for a parameter the argument each direct call hands it (`cwFnValueTargets`, `check_write_scan.vl`). A set that cannot be known (a value from a list, a field or a call result; a setter reached as a value) still writes anything, which is D5018. The frame walk also stopped walking a merged root's statements twice, which had made every module-scope name in a multi-module program look declared twice. With the hook diff applied, `vl check compiler/entry.vl` checks clean.
- The hook was priced against master (11df0cd86) and not landed: its cost is over the 0.5% bar. Both spellings build in about 40 s at each bootstrap level and reach a fixpoint, so the old `ffI32Merge` non-termination did not reproduce.
  - The setter spelling, `checkerHooksSet(check, deliver)` with fn-typed parameters, grows the seed 12,803 bytes (+0.22%) and L2 self-compile fuel 9.31% (99,883,349,859 to 109,180,531,483). It turns on monomorphisation and `fn_flow` over the compiler: `monomorphize` goes from 28 to 1,646 inclusive profile samples, and `ffBuild`/`ffWalkFn` from 0 to about 500.
  - The setter-less spelling has two `export let` slots in `check_core_state.vl`, assigned in `checkProgramPass`. It grows the seed 12,762 bytes (+0.22%), most of it the type section (+12,442), and fuel 4.37% (to 104,251,503,021).
  - That cost is the module-wide `fnValUsed` flag turning true, which enables about 67 gated emit paths. Capture analysis is about 3.1 points of it (`capComputeNamesOf`, `capScan`, `capNarrowRecordFrame`, `capCacheBuild`, `capturedKindOf`, `collectCloSigs`), measured by an unsound early return for a function with no parent frame. The other gates (`cloLiftDecide`, `blockHasCallRef` and others) are about 1.3 points.
  - The hooked seed compiling master's source spends exactly master's fuel, so the cost comes from compiling a function value, not from slower generated code. `plumb-shape-cost.vl --check` moves +22 fuel units over master's own reading.
  - What would make the hook cheap is gating closure analysis on a nested or capturing frame existing, rather than on any function value existing. The `$fnsig` type growth comes from the same flag.
  - `tests/cases/operators/overload-declared-after-use.vl` is the first fixture that reaches the two operator-force sites, `opIdxFnFor` for `[]` and `opSelfFnKeyFor` for `+`. This was checked with a compiler that traps at both sites; none of the 773 existing files that declare an operator reached either one. The hole-refill edges (`holeAssignOk`, `fillCoalHoleNodes`) are already reached by many cases.
- The closure gates are split. `fnValUsed` still means "a function value exists", and the new `fnNestUsed` means "a lambda or nested function was lifted". Capture analysis, the closure lift and unbox, captured reads and env slots, and `capCacheBuild` are gated on `fnNestUsed`.
  - Without a nested frame, `collectCloSigs` interns a function's own `$fnsig` only for a declared function named outside a call's callee, or for a function the emitter added.
  - With no nested frame, a concrete higher-order function with an un-annotated return no longer opens the mono pass, because `monoSpecializeConcrete` declines it before reading an argument. That was most of the hook's cost: the setter has fn-typed parameters and no return annotation. With a lambda present the pass still runs, because it also prunes uncalled lambdas.
  - Output identity, against the fixpoint of master: 224 modules differ, and every one runs with the same exit code and output. 219 lose 1 to 42 `$fnsig` types, 4 keep the same types in a different order, and in `modules/std-testing-fail` the trap backtrace's byte offsets move. No module grew.
  - D4891 moves from clause 1 to clause 2. Its call `fs[0](1)` is keyed as a nullary void call; master found that key only because `main`'s own sig was interned, and called through the wrong sig. The candidate does not intern it, so the same mis-keyed call is now a loud emit reject. The row stays open.
  - L2 self-compile fuel against master at 2026f3704:

    | build | fuel | seed bytes |
    | --- | --- | --- |
    | master | 98,606,856,745 | 5,919,107 |
    | master + hook | 108,985,494,771 (+10.53%) | 5,932,022 |
    | candidate | 98,487,369,569 (−0.12%) | 5,922,614 |
    | candidate + hook | 99,138,499,698 (+0.54%) | 5,922,995 |

  - The reading moves with the compiled source alone: one compiler compiling a 40-line rewrite of `collectCloSigs` read +0.52% more fuel than on the version kept. At this resolution, a difference of half a percent is noise from the source's shape.
  - `plumb-shape-cost.vl --check` fuel is lower than master: main 8,550,779,219 against 8,653,307,223 (−1.2%), and tail 6,176,765,037 against 6,249,247,227 (−1.2%). There was no re-baseline.
  - If the hook's setter gets a return annotation (`: void`), it reopens the mono pass.
- The hook landed as ONE slot, `checkerForceFn` in `check_core_state.vl`, which `checkProgramPass` installs; the force sites (`opSelfFnKeyFor`, `opIdxFnFor`, `holeAssignOk`, `fillCoalHoleNodes`) call through it, so the relation core no longer imports the core checker. Seven modules then took 17,550 lines (486 declarations) out of `typecheck.vl` (29,495 → 11,945): `check_relate_core.vl` (relation, joins, substitution, `tErr*`; 4,849), `check_annot_resolve.vl` (757), `check_fit_rules.vl` (3,667), `check_ret_name_shapes.vl` (617), `check_narrow_flow.vl` (4,130), `check_node_rules.vl` (1,544) and `check_demands.vl` (3,483).
  - The seed is 5,922,931 bytes, +317 over master. `difftree.sh --identity` found the candidate compiling master's source to master's fixpoint, and 0 of 15,273 files differ (10,317 built).

  - L2 self-compile fuel is +2.33% over master, past the 0.6% budget. The compilers are equally fast: one source compiled by master's seed and by the candidate reads the same within 0.0003%. The cost is the source's shape: the hook alone is +0.51%, and the carve adds +1.80%.
  - The carve's cost grows with the lines moved, about 0.1% per 1,000. Measured parts: the new import names (about 900, +0.26%), the new exports (243, +0.10%) and declaration order (+0.16%); the rest was not localised. So any carve of 8,000 lines or more misses a 0.6% budget, and the budget for big carves needs deciding.
  - `plumb-shape-cost.vl --check` passes, with fuel −1.2% against its baseline on both units. The per-file ladder and sentinel baselines were re-split with their totals unchanged (ladder 341 / 6, sentinel 474).
- The five re-exports `typecheck.vl` kept for `emit_mono.vl` are gone. `emit_mono.vl` imports `assignable`, `bindGenWalk`, `substTyDeep` and `tyProvesNonNull` from `check_relate_core.vl` and `ovPickFor` from `check_demands.vl`; `typecheck.vl` imports the ones it uses itself. No other file reached them through `typecheck.vl`. The fixpoint seed is byte-identical to master's (5,924,421 bytes, delta 0), and `difftree.sh --identity` reads 0 of 15,273 files differ (10,317 built).
- The fuel budget for big carves is decided: a carve may add up to +1.5% L2 self-compile fuel when the cost comes from source shape alone (more modules, more imports) and `difftree.sh --identity` reads 0 differ. Past that, the cost is found (a hot cross-module call that lost inlining, per-import-name cost in `driver.vl`) before the carve ships. The per-kind checkers then left `typecheck.vl` (11,943 → 429 lines), which keeps only `checkNode` and its dispatcher `checkNodeReal`.
  - All 88 functions left were one strongly connected component, and every cycle passed through `checkNode`, so no callee-closed carve existed. The moved code calls `checkNodeHook(ix)` (`check_core_state.vl`), which goes through the `checkerForceFn` slot `checkProgramPass` already installs (kind 0). That one-line forwarder is the only new mechanism; the rest is moves, `export`s and imports.

  - L2 self-compile fuel goes from 90,565,923,060 on master to 89,333,316,998 (−1.36%). The seed is 5,943,729 bytes, +70 over master, and it is a fixpoint (L1 == L2). `difftree.sh --identity` reads 0 of 15,276 files differ (10,320 built). `plumb-shape-cost.vl --check` passes (tail fuel −1.2%). The ladder and sentinel baselines were re-split per file with their totals unchanged (341 / 6 and 469), and a second `--write-baseline` leaves both files unchanged.
- The hook census, after the per-kind carve: 111 call sites in 12 modules reach the checker core through `checkerForceFn`, and none can be a direct import.
  - Kind 0 (`checkNode`) has 109 sites: 100 check a child node of the node being checked, 6 force a later declaration's pending return (`opSelfFnKeyFor`, `opIdxFnFor`, two in `check_calls.vl`, one each in `check_operators.vl` and `check_member_call.vl`), and 3 re-check a node rewritten in place (`check_access.vl` twice, `check_statements.vl` once). Kind 1 (`assignableExpr`) has 2: `holeAssignOk` and `fillCoalHoleNodes`.
  - Every kind-0 caller is among the 137 modules `typecheck.vl` reaches, and both kind-1 callers (`check_demands.vl`, `check_relate_core.vl`) are among the modules `check_assignable.vl` reaches. The calls are the recursion itself, so no helper move breaks a cycle: a direct edge needs the dispatcher and the per-kind checker in one module, which undoes the carve. A declaration force cannot call `checkFuncDeclNode` directly either, since it would skip `checkNode`'s tail-position, observer and recording steps.
  - Nothing in the code moved. Moving `checkerForce` and its install from `check_program.vl` into `typecheck.vl` beside `checkNode`, with no other change, cost +0.44% L2 fuel (89,918,239,511 against master's 89,520,888,450) and bought only a tidier owner for the slot, so it was dropped. Routing the two hot kind-0 reads in `check_demands.vl` and `check_relate_core.vl` through `checkNodeHook` was dropped as well. Those four reads stay direct `checkerForceFn` calls, and `checkProgramPass` still installs the slot.
  - `typecheck.vl` keeps `checkNode`, `checkNodeReal` and the install: it is the one module that imports every per-kind checker, so the dispatcher belongs there. The literal and identifier arms stay inline; the identifier arm re-dispatches an inlined `const` literal, which a lower module could reach only through the slot.
  - The comments on `checkerForceFn`, `checkNodeHook` and `checkNodeReal` now say what the slot carries and why it is a slot, and the seed is byte-identical to master's fixpoint (5,955,790 bytes).
- L9 closes (2026-10-24, measured at `4a2102699`). The item's problem was one file of 74,855 lines and 3,036 functions that 13 modules imported. `typecheck.vl` is now 511 lines (0.14% of the compiler's 363,889) with 5 functions, and 2 modules import it (`check_program.vl`, `check_emit_queries.vl`). The checker is `typecheck.vl` plus 132 `check_*.vl` modules, 108,981 lines; the largest is `check_relate_core.vl` (7,760).

Each is the body of one dispatcher arm, and carving them breaks no cycle, so they stay, as the per-kind carve decided for the literal and identifier arms.

  - ARCHITECTURE.md §7 has no L9 row. Function length is L8's (no compiler function is over 180 lines), and the size of the other checker files is not part of this item. This entry changes no code; the seed is master's.

### T2 log

- L11 the VL way: the per-file budgets move into `vl lint` itself (a baseline-reading budget mode), so the ratchets need neither Python nor spawn.
- The twin Python censuses retire as each rule's budget moves.
- **Started:** `vl check --budget` holds and writes the interp ratchet (`--budget-why`, `--write-baseline`, `--exempt-codes`; `compiler/cli_budget.vl`; #3617, #3624) and `interp-budget.py` is retired; the comment and arena-scan ratchets followed (#3648), so `vl check --budget` now holds interp, sentinel, comment and scan; the ladder, export (#3667) and render-parse (#3677) ratchets followed, so every lint-held per-file ratchet runs there and `ratchet.py` is deleted.
- **Done.**

- The last `scripts/*.py` tools are ported and deleted the same way, each run beside its Python and pinned by a test: `sentinel-census`, `wasm-runner-census`, `twin-agreement`, the four wasm fixture generators (over `scripts/lib/wasm-cases.vl`), `emit-refusal-sites`, `codemods/label-at`, and `ladder-census` and `rep-classifier-census` (over `scripts/lib/ladder-walk.vl`, which `ladder-sets.vl` now shares).
- **Done** (2026-10-24): `git ls-files` lists no `.py` file, the ratchets run under `vl check --budget` (T2), and `scripts/no-python.vl` holds the line in `gate.sh` and CI.
- Each gap found is filed as a `dogfood:` row.

### T4 log

- L10: the `-O` optimizer.
- **In progress:** rungs R1, R2, R3 and R5 (the multi-value step) run in the compiler and their Rust is deleted, and R4 (a module writer) has landed.
- R6a (the inline-record step's census) and R6b (its walk and decision) run beside the Rust.
- Next: R6c (its rewrite), then R7 (list flattening).
- **Done:** R6c to R8 moved the rest of the chain into `compiler/opt_pipe.vl`, and R9 runs it from the Deno and playground hosts byte-identically ([L10 log](#l10-log)).

### T5 log

- L12: the remaining LSP features.
- **Done:** every seed-backed feature lives in `lsp/src/features.ts` beside folding (definition, references, document highlights, document symbols, signature help, hover, inlay hints and semantic tokens in the first PR; completion, formatting, code actions, code lens and rename in the second), each taking a `FeatureContext` (checker, module key, reader, log) and answering in plain shapes.
- `server.ts` is transport: it fetches the document, calls the feature and converts to protocol types.

### T6 log

- `scripts/ci-proof.sh` checks a push run of `ci.yml` on the audited commit. It needs the same tree and a clean checkout, the same seed sha256, deno pinned by `.dvmrc`, the same rustc, and every job passed with every shard present. With `GATE_CI_PROOF=<sha>`, `gate.sh` then skips the 15 rows that CI runs, and the corpus oracle. That is about 1,100 of the gate's 1,750–1,900 CPU-s. With the variable unset, the gate behaves as before. `tests/ci_proof_test.ts` refuses 28 mutated inputs.

### L12 log

- The playground adapter is a transport over `lsp/src/features.ts`, as `server.ts` is: `lspAdapter.ts` 614 → 389 lines. Where the two hosts disagreed, the shared behaviour was kept: hover's effect summary, UFCS completions with import edits, the protocol's code-action kind hierarchy, seed folding extents, `=>` token fusion and `\r\n` word scans. `tests/playground_lsp_parity_test.ts` now asserts that both hosts call each row's shared function. Code lens stays out because the playground keeps no use-map.

The 128 most-referenced types take indices 0–127, where a type index is one LEB byte, and among them the most-referenced as a heap type or block type take 0–63, where that s33 is one byte. Every other type keeps its relative order, and a supertype still precedes its subtypes. The rec group is the only reordered unit, the standalone function types after it keep their indices, and a module of 64 group types or fewer is left byte-identical. ### S5 log

- Step 4: `compiler/iface_hash.vl` computes a per-module interface hash and skips nothing yet. It covers the tokens with each fully annotated, non-generic body cut out, the names each cut body mentions and how, every top-level binding's checked type, and a deep hash over imports. Under Q4 (A), generic, untyped-parameter and inferred-return bodies stay in. `tests/vl_iface_hash_test.ts` grades 30 single-edit pairs with 0 unsound and 8 hits, and dropping each of the six ingredients turns its witness unsound. The history hit rate is 2 of 15 compiler edits (13.3%), 4 of 15 with inferred-return bodies cut, both with 0 unsound. Seed 6,168,691 → 6,181,799 bytes. Next: bank the edited module's importer demands (Q1 B adoption, D3817 parameters, instantiations) so that a dependent's re-check can be skipped.
- Step 4b: the interface hash cuts inferred-return bodies and lets their checked type carry them, with the body folded back in when the type renders elided. It hashes only the statements a dependent can reach: exports, top-level `let`s and statements, and whatever their tokens or checked types name, plus the mention rows of the private functions an exposed body calls. Still inert, and no annotation is asked of users (Q4 A). The suite grades 67 pairs with 32 hits and 0 unsound, against 10 hits for the step-4 hash, and each of six new ingredients has a mutation witness. Single-function edits from history keep the hash at 25.0% (was 12.5%) on the compiler, 76.2% (47.6%) on diderven, 66.7% (54.8%) on eardstap and 90.1% (80.2%) on sunpa, all with 0 unsound. Seed 6,199,146 → 6,212,449 bytes. Next: bank importer demands.
- Step 5: `checkSkipArm` (driver.vl, unarmed by default and by every caller) banks a module check: each module's source, its six hash readings, its diagnostics with rendered messages and module-relative ends, and its lint pairing rows. The next check merges only S, the changed modules closed over their imports. A module outside S is served from the bank when every name it imports from S is closed: a type, an extern, an annotated binding, a string or boolean binding, a scalar-literal `const`, or one non-generic function with annotated, undefaulted parameters. After the check, each served edge into S must keep its deep hash, every fresh diagnostic must lie in S, and S's spans must hold when a served module has a diagnostic; otherwise the whole check runs again. Generic, inferred-parameter, defaulted, numeric-binding and overloaded imports, an entry re-export from S, and an edit to the entry all decline. While armed, diagnostics are listed in module order. `tests/vl_check_skip_test.ts` grades 26 edits twice each (main clean and with errors): 19 serve and 33 decline, and every one gives the whole check's diagnostics row for row and a byte-identical build after it. It caught a banked `#lu` message rendering against the next check's tables. Two mutation witnesses (`ifaceHashDrop` 80 and 16) serve unsoundly. On the compiler graph, a body edit to `fmt_util.vl` serves 212 of 217 modules, at 0.95 s against 3.4–3.7 s for a whole check (median of 5, load about 20). An edit to `check_overload.vl` declines on the entry's re-exports. Seed 6,454,136 → 6,462,627 bytes; L2 fuel +1.7% on baseline.
- Step 6: `createWasmChecker`/`loadWasmChecker` take `checkSkip`, and only the Node LSP server passes it; the playground and every CLI check stay unarmed. `recheck` runs `checkSrc` (which can serve), unlike `checkSrcSym`, which banks but never serves. The server records which keys each open document's check read. `recheckImporters`, which runs in the save and 3 s idle pass, re-checks a document when another key in its graph was edited after its check. Before this, an importer kept its stale diagnostics until it was itself edited. The bank holds up to `SK_PARK_MAX` (4) entries' banks (`skSelect`), so checking another entry keeps this one's bank. Diagnostic order: across the differential suite's 58 checks, 0 unarmed checks list their rows in another order than `diagOrderKey`. The LSP stable-sorts by it anyway. `skClosed` admits a numeric-literal binding whose checked type kept its hash. Tests: `tests/vl_check_skip_test.ts` adds two-entry banking and lint-after-skip (the served lint equals the whole one, and an unpaired lint differs), and `tests/lsp_check_skip_wasm_test.ts` grades a body edit and a lib-error edit (both served) and a signature edit (whole), each against an unarmed checker. Measured on `compiler/entry.vl` after a body-only edit of `fmt_util.vl`: the entry re-check takes 3.5–3.6 s whole and 0.97–1.19 s served (212 modules served). An entry edit, never served, costs the same armed as unarmed (3.8–4.1 s). Seed 6,469,034 → 6,471,019 bytes (+1,985). L2 self-compile guest fuel is 103,159,869,610 (+1.2% on its baseline).
- Step 6 widening: three changes to `skSettle` and `skPlan` (driver.vl).
  - Spans relocate. A banked diagnostic already stores its end relative to its own module, so moving S's tokens moves nothing it reads, unless the diagnostic can name another module's position. `skRelocatable` reads it as safe when its span starts inside its own module's tokens and neither its message nor its data spells a number, other than a width name such as `i32`. `SK_SPAN` now declines only when such a non-relocatable diagnostic is served and some S module's token line/column hash (`ihPosHash`, banked as `skPosH`) moved, not on any change to S's length.
  - The re-export decline (`SK_REEXPORT`, `skReExported`) is gone. The entry's export surface is the entry's own check, and the interface hash already covers the re-exported declaration, so a body edit under a re-export serves and a signature edit declines for interface.
  - `skClosed` answers three ways. An un-annotated, non-scalar `let` is `SK_IF_PLAIN`, and `skSettle` admits it after the check when `ihPlainBinding` (iface_hash.vl) holds: the checked type resolves, holds no function (`tyMayHoldFn`) and no type variable, and does not render elided. Why that is sound: the binding's whole-check type is a join over every module's uses, and the deep hash on the served→S edge banked that type. If the S-only check gives the same type, the served modules' unchanged uses fit it again, so the whole check would give that type too. A function type is excluded because a call re-checks the lambda body at the caller's types; an elided rendering is excluded because equal strings would not mean equal types.
  - Left out: generic functions, hole (unannotated) parameters and defaulted parameters stay `SK_OPEN`. An importer's call of one re-checks an instance, and `plBankSites` folds that instance's lint sites into the origin module's. Per-call checker state (L1 per-call binding, L3 hole calls) lets an importer's arguments reach the origin's own diagnostics, and a default expression is cloned and checked in the caller. None of these is in a hash or in a bank reading, so serving the importer could differ from a whole check.
  - Tests: `tests/vl_check_skip_test.ts` has 36 cases, each graded twice (15 serve, 18 decline, 3 either). New in this PR:
    - two span cases, a same-length edit under a numbered diagnostic (serves) and a line-shifting one (declines for span);
    - a re-export signature edit (declines);
    - an inferred record binding read across a body edit (serves);
    - a lib body that starts adopting that binding at `i64`, and a main that adopts it (both decline for interface);
    - a function-valued binding (declines for demand).

    The new mutation witness drops types and mentions (`8 | 16`) on the adopting edit; it serves and differs.
  - Replay of compiler/'s last 30 commits as edits to `compiler/entry.vl`'s graph (217 modules), each check against an unarmed one:
    - before: 2 `all`, 28 `re-export`;
    - after the span and re-export changes: 2 `all`, 28 `demand`;
    - after the binding widening: 2 `all`, 28 `interface`.

    0 of 30 are served at each stage, and 0 are unsound. Each interface decline traces to the per-module granularity of the deep hash. A served module imports a handful of names from an S module, but any exposed change there moves the hash. For example, a lib whose exported `g` starts calling a new private `h` declines a main that imports only `f`, because `g`'s mention rows moved. The next widening is a per-imported-name demand, which needs its own soundness argument covering lbi joins, record adoption and global writes through callees.
  - Seed 6,481,412 → 6,483,820 bytes (+2,408) on #4106's tree. Measured on #4102's tree, L2 self-compile guest fuel is 103,886,188,333 with the candidate seed and 103,886,189,026 with the pre-change seed on the same source, so the change costs nothing on the unarmed path.
- Step 7: a per-imported-name reach replaces the per-module deep hash. `ihReachOf(i, o)` (iface_hash.vl) covers the names importer `i` imports from `o`, with the count of `o`'s statements carrying each. It closes them over the identifiers and checked-type names they spell, down `o`'s imports, and then over the bodies they call. A body that may write through a reference gives its whole tokens. `skBankNow` banks one reach per (importer, declaring module) pair, and `skSettle` declines only when a served importer's reach moved. Three global guards are new: `ambient` (a served module holds a type, a structural spelling or a receiver function while S is checked, or an S module's such statements moved), `fresh` (a served importer reaches an inferred non-scalar return), and `decor` (S's declarations moved while a served diagnostic is banked). The soundness argument, case by case, is design doc §5 "S5 step 7". **Master's skip is unsound on 8 suite edits**: record adoption across modules (4) and a callee's write that ends a narrowing (4). Each is served with a diagnostic missing, and this step declines all 8. `tests/vl_check_skip_test.ts` grades 56 edits (18 serve, 35 decline, 3 either), 0 unsound. The new ingredients have six mutation witnesses (`16384` ×3 and `32768` ×3). Armed under the `-O` flavour, the linked module equals the direct one: self-build 226 artifacts and 20,365 rows, tests/cases 5,086 modules, 6,099 artifacts and 56,250 rows, 0 differ. The emitter's `-O` flavour is five readers of `gOptimized` and `gLowMemUnused` (the map-set helper, the pooled-string fill, low-memory addressing). It changes 782 of 4,315 single-file case modules and 1,444 of 6,099 armed case artifacts, so it stays in the emitter and joins the artifact key; moving it behind the link would change `-O` output. `tests/vl_opt_after_link_test.ts` pins the closure, the five readers, and the chain run count (0 plain, 1 for `-O`, `-O3` and `-O -O3`); two import controls red. No compiler source changed: the seed is byte-identical to master's fixpoint (5,676,488 bytes), two refreshes agree, L2 fuel 0. Plumb-shape cost passes (main fuel −3.1%, tail −3.2%, RSS −2.2% against its baseline).
- D7500: per-module serving was measured and dropped, with no compiler change. In an unseen root's check, the body of an annotated, non-generic, top-level import function was skipped when the bank already held its module with every source unchanged. `wiGuard` then ran again on each skipped module's closure, with the root as the outside. On diderven's `vl check src` guest fuel went 115.80G → 116.13G at 1 job (1,697 modules skipped, 1,313 of them then declined `ambient`, so 46 of 65 roots would need a re-check) and 132.37G → 137.65G at 2 jobs (554 skipped, 95 declined). The bound without the bank gate is −16.4% for annotated bodies and −23% for every body, at 2 jobs. Neither is sound: a skipped body that pins an empty list hides `push: cannot add i32 to string[]`, and banks built from such checks lose three hints. The measurements and both counter-programs are in D7500. Next: D7501, because `ambient` bounds per-module serving just as it bounds whole-root serving.

The root's own check knows a subset of the merged check's names. A one-word render whose word nothing outside G declares therefore names a primitive or a G declaration, and the root's check renders the same word. No declaration names a bare array, so `W[]` renders as its element does.

### R log

- First round: 66 `kind-ladder-incomplete` and 41 `sentinel-index-unguarded` hits are paid in five files outside the other lanes. Each ladder is a `_`-less `match` over its whole kind set, with the declined kinds named in one arm, and each sentinel read is bound-tested or goes through a guarded reader (`hcLenOf`; `rtAlloc` and `rtCompleteChild` write the rep-tree arena). The ratchets go 341 → 275 (`kind-ladder-split` stays 6) and 467 → 426. Identity over the difftree population reads 0 differ, two refreshes give the same seed, and `vl check --severity info` adds no finding. The seed shrinks 6,236,629 → 6,235,950 bytes, and L2 guest fuel is +0.001% against master's seed over the same tree. The sentinel count's rise from 300 to 461 at #3867, and 455 → 474 at #3902, is detector widening: every earlier tree reads the same count under the current detector, and the per-message multisets before and after #3867 are equal.
- Second round: 94 `kind-ladder-incomplete`, 2 `kind-ladder-split` and 101 `sentinel-index-unguarded` hits are paid in fourteen files outside the other lanes, `emit_rep.vl`'s three residue ladders and `tySameAt`'s two among them. `tySameAt` is one `match x` whose arms each hold an exhaustive `match y`, because the lint counts a second subject's tests across arms. The ratchets go 275 → 181, 6 → 4 and 426 → 325. No guard reached a live trap: each bound test covers an index that would already trap, so no row is filed. Identity over the difftree population reads 0 differ, two refreshes give the same seed, and `vl check --severity info` against master's tree removes exactly those 197 hits and adds none.
